# Žubrinić households, name chains and tables (Otočac, 1780-1872)

Built from `sources/otocac-zubrinic-table.tsv` (1279 rows) after the events pass (`events.tsv`, 938 events, event ids given as `X16-D012` etc.) with the strip-report corrections and the zoom checks applied. Names are as written; modern forms only in parentheses. Nothing is inferred beyond what a record says. No living people appear (latest entry 1872).

Full-zoom check, 10 November 2026 (made after this file was built; the households and chains below have NOT been rebuilt from it). The 165 medium and low table rows were re-read at level 13; the verdicts are in the table's `verified` column and each event's `status` in `events.tsv` is now `zoom_confirmed`, `zoom_stem`, `zoom_unsettled` or `zoom_lookalike` where one of its table rows was zoomed. Read an event below as NOT established if it has `zoom_lookalike` (the name is another name: N3B-B059, N3B-B115, N3B-B120, N3B-B132, N3B-B135, N3B-B149, Y4C-B235, X16-M008, X16-M010) or `zoom_unsettled` (27 events, listed in `events.tsv`). The high-confidence rows are first-pass readings, not re-zoomed.


Conventions. House = village plus house number as read; a village name is normalised (Grozor/Prosor = Prozor, Shumec/Šumeć = Šumećica, Police/Paljice = Poljice) while the original spelling stays in `events.tsv`. `(?)` after a number = number read as doubtful or alternative. "also at" = the entry names a second house. Status tags show only when not `read_by_reader`. An event whose house number is blank or illegible is in section 1.3, not in a household. The 1780s X16 deaths carry a "Serie/Numero" that is not a house number; they are not used here.

## 1. Households (place and house number)

115 households (village+number) have at least one Žubrinić event; 412 event-house lines. Events that name two houses appear under both. Relation tag: **Ž household** = a Žubrinić is the principal, father, spouse or parent; **married-into** = a Žubrinić is the mother née; **other family's house** = a Žubrinić stood as godparent or witness; **Ž godparent's own house** = the entry gives the godparent's house.

422 events have no village or house in the readings (mostly the 1780-1836 X16 deaths and the N3B/Y4C entries whose house column is blank). They are in `events.tsv` and are counted in section 4; they cannot be placed.

## 2. People named Žubrinić, by given name

A **hard link** is made only where (a) the same canonical given name (Ioannes = Ivan, Matthaeus = Mate and so on, from the Atlas given-name families) occurs with the **same numbered house**, with the chain cut at a death entry, at a gap of over 25 years between mentions, where one entry names two people of that name, and where a death age puts the birth after an earlier mention as an adult; or (b) the register itself says "uxor of", "filius of" or "defunctus". Rule (a) is a rule, not a proof: it asserts nothing the register does not support, but two men of one name in one house within 25 years (father and son) would be merged. Where the chain is a likely father/son, the lines say so. Children are never put in an adult chain; a child baptism is joined to a death only in 2.2. Mothers née Ž and godparents are not in these chains (their house is the family they married into or stood for).

Mentions used: 711 adult Žubrinić-cell mentions, of which 325 sit in a chain with a house number (186 chains; 59 with two or more mentions); 386 have no single reliable house; 33 deaths have no readable age (kept out of chains); 445 child mentions.

## 4. Events per decade and volume

Events (not rows). A death entered in both X16 and Y4C (nine, March 1834-August 1836) counts once in each volume column and once in the total. X16 census index (15 household heads, no year) and the 4 look-alikes are not counted.

| decade | N3B bapt. | Y4C bapt. | Y4C deaths | X16 deaths | X16 marr. | events (distinct) |

| 1780s | 0 | 0 | 0 | 55 | 0 | 55 |
| 1790s | 0 | 0 | 0 | 30 | 0 | 30 |
| 1800s | 0 | 0 | 0 | 15 | 0 | 15 |
| 1810s | 0 | 0 | 0 | 29 | 0 | 29 |
| 1820s | 0 | 0 | 0 | 43 | 0 | 43 |
| 1830s | 97 | 0 | 30 | 39 | 0 | 157 |
| 1840s | 134 | 61 | 60 | 0 | 0 | 255 |

Coverage limits behind the zeros: N3B is births 1834-46; Y4C is births 1846-58 and deaths 1834-58; X16 is deaths 1780-1836, marriages 1859-72 and deaths 1859-72, so no X16 deaths for 1837-58 (Y4C covers deaths then), no baptism before 1834, no marriage before 1859. A blank decade is a gap in the volumes, not an absence of the family.
